Use when designing types, schemas, state machines, or any structure where invalid states should be unrepresentable. Uses Hamming distance to measure change locality and delta-epsilon to characterize the exact boundary where a change crosses into another semantic domain. Respects the domain partial order: drift in a prerequisite domain invalidates dependent domains. Use when reviewing diffs, auditing generated code, or verifying refactors.
